```python
    def eval_raw(a):
       a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if a.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return np.inf
       try: return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
       except Exception: return np.inf
```

```python

Fsize=_p2(Lconv)
Gsize=_p2(3*n-2)
```
n=6962 => 16384,32768.

Functions:
```python
def fft_conv(a, size=Fsize):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
Smoothing:
```python
def smooth_obj_grad(a, temp, exclude_zero=True):
    C=fft_conv(a)
    if exclude_zero:
        idx=1
        arr=C[1:]
    else:
        idx=0; arr=C
    M=max(arr)
    z=temp*(arr-M)
    # logsumexp stable: z max 0; max z 0.
    # exp(z) safe if temp*(arr-M)<0 no overflow. Underflow okay.
    ez=np.exp(z)
    sumez=ez.sum()
    if sumez<=0: w_off=1/len(arr); logsum=log(len(arr))
    else: w_off=ez/sumez; logsum=math.log(sumez)
    Msm=M + logsum/temp
    # weights full length
    w=np.zeros(Lconv)
    w[idx:]=w_off  # if idx=1, length Lconv-1
    # gradient
    wf=w[::-1]
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, Gsize)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(wf, Gsize)
    conv2=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, Gsize)[:3*n-2]
    dM=2.0*conv2[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    S=a.sum()
    if S<=0: Msm, invS2, grad = inf...
    invS2=1/(S*S)
    grad=(2.0*n)*invS2*dM - (4.0*n*Msm)*invS2/S * np.ones(n) (or array)
    return Msm, grad
```

```python
def optimize_nontrivial(a0, seconds, temp_start, temp_end, lr0, tag):
    a = norm_a(np.clip(a0,0,1000), target=float(n))
    sc = eval_exact_nonneg(a) # maybe evaluator
    update best
    m = zeros; v=zeros; step=0
    start = time.monotonic()
    last_eval = start - eval_interval
    while time < deadline and elapsed < seconds:
        temp = linear or exponential from start to end based on progress = elapsed/seconds
        # maybe add small noise? Could restart if no improvement.
        C = fft_conv(a)
        C_off = C[1:]
        # If C_off all nan? no
        M = float(C_off.max())
        z = temp*(C_off - M)
        ez = np.exp(z)
        sumez = ez.sum()
        if not finite or sumez<=0: weights uniform
        else w_off = ez/sumez; logsum=math.log(sumez)
        Msm = M + logsum/temp if temp>0 else M
        w = zeros(Lconv); w[1:] = w_off
        # maybe symmetrize weights? Since C symmetric, w_off from C_off not exactly symmetric due to FFT numerical. The gradient formula with nonsymmetric weights may introduce bias. We can enforce w = 0.5*(w + w[::-1])? But w[0]=0; w_off indexes 1..L-1. Symmetrizing ensures derivative corresponds to max over symmetric set? If C symmetric, softmax weights symmetric if values exactly. FFT roundoff tiny. Symmetrize: w[1:]=w_off; ws=0.5*(w + w[::-1]); ws[0]=0; ws[1:]/=ws[1:].sum()? But symmetrizing excludes zero; after sym, sum maybe same? We can do:
        w_full = np.zeros(Lconv); w_full[1:] = w_off
        w_full = 0.5*(w_full + w_full[::-1])
        w_full[0]=0
        wsum = w_full.sum()
        if wsum>0: w_full /= wsum
```

        # gradient
        wf = w[::-1]
        fa = rfft(a, Gsize); fw = rfft(wf, Gsize)
        conv2 = irfft(fa*fw, Gsize)[:3n-2]
        dM = 2.0*conv2[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
        S = a.sum()
        invS2 = 1/(S*S)
        grad = (2*n)*invS2*dM - (4*n*Msm)*invS2/S * np.ones(n)
        # Normalize gradient? optional
        # To reduce scale effect with target n, grad magnitude ~1/n? Actually with target n, grad ~4/n. Adam ok.
        # Adaptive clipping? Use Adam
        step +=1
        m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*grad
        v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*(grad*grad)
        mb = m/(1-beta1**step)
        vb = v/(1-beta2**step)
        # lr schedule: lr = lr0 * gamma^(step/decay_steps). gamma 0.92 per 1000? Use exp(-decay*step)
        lr = lr0 * (0.9^(step/2000.0)) or 0.999?
        a = a - lr * mb/(sqrt(vb)+eps)
        a = clip and norm
        # periodic exact eval with hidden evaluator to update best
        if step % eval_every ==0 or elapsed-last_eval>eval_sec:
            sc=eval_raw(a.tolist())
            if sc<best: update
            last_eval=now
        # occasional random perturbation? maybe if stuck.
    final eval

Direct iteration:
```python
fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Gsize)
C = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, Gsize)[:L]
M = C.max()
z = temp*(C - M)
ez = np.exp(z)
wsum = ez.sum()
if wsum <=0 or not finite: w = np.zeros(L); w[argmax]=1
else w = ez/wsum
Msm = M + log(wsum)/temp
wf = w[::-1]
fw = np.fft.rfft(wf, Gsize)
conv2 = np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, Gsize)[:3n-2]
dM = 2.0*conv2[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
S = a.sum()
invS2 = 1/(S*S)
grad = 2*n*invS2*dM - 4*n*Msm*invS2/S
# Note: if S not exactly target due to normalization, okay.
```

Euclidean projection onto simplex:
```python
def project_simplex(v, s):
    # v shape n
    if len(v)==0: return v
    # If s <=0 return zeros
    # We need q in [0,∞) sum=s minimizing ||q-v||.
    # Algorithm: sort v desc; find rho such that sum_{v_i>theta}(v_i-theta)=s.
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, n+1) > (cssv - s))[0]
    if len(rho)==0: return np.zeros_like(v)
    theta = (cssv[rho[0]] - s) / (rho[0]+1)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0)
```
This is O(n log n). For n=6962, sorting 7k ~0.0003s; inner 300 ->0.1s, fine. Use this for inner convex minimization. def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):

Exact eval function:
```python
def eval_candidate(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0: return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, cap)
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 0.01: return np.inf
    # finite
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return np.inf
    try:
       sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
       return sc if np.isfinite(sc) else np.inf
    except: return np.inf
```
But converting 6962 floats to list every eval maybe overhead but okay. For many internal exact updates, use FFT score to avoid list conversion. But final/periodic use evaluator. FFT score:
```python
def score_fft(a):
    S=a.sum()
    if S<0.01: return inf
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, Fsize); c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, Fsize)[:L]
    M=float(c.max()) if c.size else inf
    if M<0: M=0
    return 2.0*n*M/(S*S)
```
Since target sum n, S≈n. Use this for fast best? But final evaluator may differ due to clipping/scaling. We can use FFT for surrogate but update best with evaluator every N.

Direct optimizer function:
```python
def direct_optimize(a0, seconds, temp_start, temp_end, lr0, lr_end, eval_every=400, tag=''):
    a = project_simplex(a0, target)
    ensure cap
    sc = eval_candidate(a); if sc<best: update (nonlocal holder)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); step=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    last_eval=start
    best_local=a.copy(); best_local_score=sc
    while time<deadline and (time-start)<seconds:
        progress = min(1, (time-start)/max(1e-6, seconds))
        temp = temp_start * (temp_end/temp_start) ** progress
        # maybe if C spread? We'll just schedule.
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a, Gsize)
        C=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, Gsize)[:L]
        Mc = float(C.max())
        if not finite: break
        z=temp*(C-Mc)
        # numerical stable
        ez=np.exp(z)
        ssum=ez.sum()
        if not finite or ssum<=0:
           w=np.zeros(L); idx=int(np.argmax(C)); w[idx]=1.0
        else:
           w=ez/ssum
        logsum = math.log(float(ssum)) if ssum>0 else 0
        Msm = Mc + logsum/temp if temp>0 else Mc
        wf=w[::-1]
        fw=np.fft.rfft(wf, Gsize)
        conv2=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, Gsize)[:3*n-2]
        dM = 2.0*conv2[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
        S = float(a.sum())
        invS2 = 1.0/(S*S)
        grad = (2.0*n)*invS2*dM - (4.0*n*Msm)*invS2/S
        # if grad has nan, break
        step_local = step+1
        m = 0.9*m + 0.1*grad
        v = 0.999*v + 0.001*(grad*grad)
        mb = m/(1-0.9**step_local)
        vb = v/(1-0.999**step_local)
        lr = lr0 * (lr_end/lr0) ** progress # or decay
        a = project_simplex(a - lr * mb/(np.sqrt(vb)+1e-8), target)
        # cap safe: if a.max()>cap: a=np.minimum(a,cap); a=project_simplex(a,target) maybe loop
        if a.max()>cap:
            a = np.minimum(a, cap)
            a = project_simplex(a, target)
            # if still >cap, maybe flatten large: a=np.minimum(a, cap*0.9); a=project_simplex(a,target)
        # periodic exact eval
        if step_local % eval_every == 0 or time-last_eval > 1.0:
            sc = eval_candidate(a)
            if sc < holder[1]: update
            # also update local best
            last_eval=time.monotonic()
    final eval
    return a
```

```python
def optimize_g(f, seconds, temp0, temp1, lr0, eval_g_every=50):
    g = f.copy()
    g = project_simplex(g, target)
    ff = np.fft.rfft(f, Gsize)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); step=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    best_g = g.copy(); best_J = None
    while time<deadline and elapsed<seconds:
       fg = np.fft.rfft(g, Gsize)
       y = np.fft.irfft(ff*fg, Gsize)[:L]
       M = y.max()
       z = temp*(y-M)
       w = softmax
       # If f*g linear, Jsm=M+logsum/temp
       wf=w[::-1]
       fw=np.fft.rfft(wf, Gsize)
       conv2=np.fft.irfft(ff*fw, Gsize)[:3n-2]
       D=conv2[n-1:2n-1][::-1]
       step+=1; adam; g=project_simplex(g - lr*..., target)
       if step%...: J= y.max? compute exact from f*g; update
    return normalized best_g, best_J
```

Implementation of active LP:
```python
def lp_candidate(f, K, max_seconds):
    # f sum target
    C = conv FFT f*f
    active = np.argsort(C)[-K:]  # highest
    A = np.zeros((K,n))
    for r,j in enumerate(active):
       lo=max(0,j-n+1); hi=min(j,n-1)
       start=j-lo; end=j-hi
       if end>0: A[r,lo:hi+1] = f[start:end-1:-1]
       else: A[r,lo:hi+1] = f[start::-1]
    # LP min t subject A g - t <=0, sum g = target, g>=0, t>=0
    c = np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1
    A_ub = np.hstack((A, -np.eye(K)))
    b_ub = np.zeros(K)
    A_eq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1
    b_eq = np.array([target])
    bounds = [(0,None)]*n + [(0,None)]
    res = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'time_limit': max(5, max_seconds)})
    if res.success:
       g = res.x[:n]
       g[g<0]=0
       if g.sum()>0: g*=target/g.sum()
       return g
    return None
```

Define:
```python
def simplex_capsafe(v, target, cap=1000):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.clip(v, 0, cap)
    # If sum too low or too high, project to target while respecting cap?
    # Use project_simplex then cap, repeat.
    for _ in range(10):
        s = v.sum()
        if s <= 0: break
        if s != target: v = v*(target/s)
        if v.max() <= cap: break
        v = np.minimum(v, cap)
        # If sum now target? no, scale again
    # If still sum not target or max>cap, use project_simplex on v (which may exceed cap)
    v = project_simplex(v, target)
    if v.max() > cap:
       # distribute excess from capped to lower coordinates
       # iterative: cap=900 to ensure room
       for cap_inner in (900,800,700,500):
           vv=np.minimum(v, cap_inner)
           vv = project_simplex(vv, target)
           if vv.max() <= cap: return vv
       # last: make constant
       return np.full(n, target/n, dtype=np.float64)
    return v
```

```python
def project_capsafe(v, target, cap=900):
    v = np.clip(v, 0, cap)
    for _ in range(8):
        v = project_simplex(v, target)
        if v.max() <= cap and np.isfinite(v).all(): return v
        v = np.minimum(v, cap)
    # If total sum of capped vector < target, impossible under cap? Since cap*n >= target (cap 900 > n? yes for n=6962 cap 900, max total capped 6.2M > target), so project should succeed.
```

Now, implement `_project_simplex` carefully to handle target and finite.
```python
def _project_simplex(v, s):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0: return v
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    if s <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    # If v sum already s and min>=0, return copy? but still maybe negative.
    # Standard algorithm requires nonnegative output.
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    # rho = max i such that u_i - (cssv_i - s)/i > 0
    cs = np.arange(1, n+1, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.nonzero(u * cs > (cssv - s))[0]
    if len(idx) == 0:
        # all u <=0? then distribute equally? But if s>0 and v very negative, projection should be uniform s/n? Actually projection of negative vector to simplex: if all v <=0, theta=(sum v -s)/n <0, q = v-theta, some positive. The condition maybe fails? Let's handle by returning uniform if no idx? But uniform may not be closest if some v positive small. The formula: if no idx, theta = (cssv[-1]-s)/n? Wait cssv[-1]=sum v. But if all u_i <= (cssv_i-s)/i, then theta=(sum v -s)/n, q=max(v-theta,0) = uniform? Let's derive. If no idx, theta = (cssv[n]-s)/n = (sum v -s)/n; since u_i <= ... maybe all q positive? In standard, if no idx, theta = (cssv[n]-s)/n; return v-theta (all nonnegative). That yields sum s. Use that instead of uniform.
        theta = (float(cssv[-1]) - s) / n
        return v - theta
    i = idx[0]
    theta = (cssv[i] - s) / (i + 1)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
```
Need ensure output sum s within tolerance; due to floating, can renormalize `out *= s/out.sum()` after? But that may break nonnegative? okay. Add:
```python
out = np.maximum(v-theta,0)
so = out.sum()
if so>0: out *= s/so
```
This may slightly alter but okay.

If `v` has huge values, theta huge; fine. If `s` > n*1000? target n <= cap*n (cap 1000) so possible. For n=6962, s=6962<<6M.

Project capsafe:
```python
def _project_capsafe(v, s, cap=1000.0):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, cap)
    out = _project_simplex(v, s)
    for _ in range(8):
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, cap)
        out = _project_simplex(out, s)
        if out.max() <= cap:
            break
    if out.max() > cap or not np.all(np.isfinite(out)):
        # fallback: cap, add deficit to non-full
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, cap)
        out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
        for _ in range(10):
            deficit = s - out.sum()
            if abs(deficit) < 1e-9 or deficit <= 0: break
            if deficit > 0:
                room = (cap - out)
                total_room = room.sum()
                if total_room <= 0:
                    out = np.full(out.size, s/out.size)
                    break
                add = np.minimum(room, np.maximum(deficit,0) * out.size / total_room?)
```
Simpler fallback: set uniform `s/n` (<= cap because cap=1000, s/n=1). This is valid though loses progress. Use only if cap issues.
```
return out
```
But if out sum after project maybe not s due to clip? `_project_simplex` returns sum s. Clipping again may reduce. Loop. Uniform fallback safe.

Now, `score_fft` for candidate after capsafe:
```python
def score_fast(a):
    S=a.sum()
    if S <= 0: return inf
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Fsize)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, Fsize)[:L]
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M): return inf
    if M < 0: M=0
    return float(2.0*n*M/(S*S))
```
Because we use Gsize for direct, for zeroth? Use Fsize smaller. If n=1, Fsize=1? `_p2(1)=1`, rfft size1 works? `np.fft.rfft` with n=1 returns size1. Good. `Gsize=_p2(3*n-2)=_p2(1)=1`.

But for gradient size `3*n-2`, for n=1 =>1. Slices `n-1:2n-1` => 0:1, good. `wf` length L=1. OK.

Direct iteration with n=1: C length1, M = a0^2, gradients? Objective scale invariant constant; fine. But we won't have n=1 likely.

`_p2` for l=0? We call with positive. If l=1, (1-1).bit_length=0, 1<<0=1.

```python
def run_direct(a0, seconds, temp0, temp1, lr0, lr1, tag):
    a = _project_capsafe(np.asarray(a0,dtype), target, 900)
    sc = _eval_exact(a)
    if sc < holder[1]: holder = a.copy(), sc
    m = zeros; v=zeros; step=0; start=monotonic(); last_exact=start
    best_fft_sc = score_fast(a); best_fft_a=a.copy()
    lr_mult=1.0; no_improve=0
    while time<deadline and (time-start)<max(1,seconds):
        # if no exact improvement for e.g. 20s, maybe add small noise? Could restart from best_fft.
        if time-start > 30 and time-last_noise? maybe every 60s if no_improve>10: a = best_fft_a + 0.01*noise projected
        progress = min(1.0, (time-start)/seconds)
        temp = temp0 * (temp1/temp0)**progress
        fa = rfft(a, Gsize)
        C = irfft(fa*fa, Gsize)[:L]
        Mc = C.max()
        # If score_fast current improves best_fft
        S = a.sum()
        sfft = 2*n*Mc/(S*S) if S>0 else inf
        if sfft < best_fft_sc - 1e-9:
            best_fft_sc = sfft; best_fft_a = a.copy()
        z = temp*(C-Mc)
        ez = np.exp(z); wsum = ez.sum()
        if not finite or wsum<=0:
            w = zeros(L); w[argmax]=1
        else: w=ez/wsum
        Msm = Mc + math.log(wsum)/temp
        wf = w[::-1]; fw = rfft(wf, Gsize)
        conv2 = irfft(fa*fw, Gsize)[:3*n-2]
        dM = 2.0*conv2[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
        # If dM nan, break
        step +=1
        m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*dM
        v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*(dM*dM)
        mb = m/(1-beta1**step)
        vb = v/(1-beta2**step)
        lr = (lr0 * (lr1/lr0)**progress) * lr_mult
        a = _project_capsafe(a - lr * mb/(np.sqrt(vb)+1e-8), target, 900)
        # exact check
        if time-start - last_exact > 1.5 or step % 500 ==0:
            sc0 = _eval_exact(a)
            if sc0 < holder[1]: holder=a.copy(), sc0; no_improve=0
            else: no_improve+=1
            scB = _eval_exact(best_fft_a) # maybe if different
            if scB < holder[1]: holder=best_fft_a.copy(), scB
            last_exact = time-start
            # adapt lr_mult
            if no_improve >= 4:
                lr_mult *= 0.7
                no_improve = 0
            # maybe restart from best_fft_a if no exact improvement 5?
    final exact
    return a
```

```python
       progress = ...
       temp=...

       z=temp*(y-M); w=softmax
```

Line search update:
```python
def mix_update(f, g, alphas):
    s = score_fast(f)
    best_h = f; best_s = s
    for alpha in alphas:
        h = (1-alpha)*f + alpha*g
        h = _project_capsafe(h, target, 900)
        sc = score_fast(h)
        if sc < best_s - 1e-12:
            best_s=sc; best_h=h.copy()
    if best_s < score_fast(f):
        sc_exact = _eval_exact(best_h)
        if sc_exact < holder[1]: holder=best_h.copy(), sc_exact
    return best_h
```

Let's detail `_eval_exact`:
```python
def _eval_exact(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0: return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    try:
        sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
        return sc if np.isfinite(sc) else np.inf
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
```

Let's draft `run_direct` fully:
```python
def run_direct(a0, seconds, temp0, temp1, lr0, lr1, tag=''):
    nonlocal? In nested, holder list; can modify.
    a = _project_capsafe(np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).copy(), target, 900.0)
    sc = _eval_exact(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < holder[1]:
        holder[0] = a.copy(); holder[1] = sc
    if seconds <= 0 or time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline:
        return a
    m = np.zeros(n); v = np.zeros(n)
    step = 0
    start = time.monotonic()
    last_exact = start
    best_fft_sc = _score_fast(a)
    best_fft_a = a.copy()
    lr_mult = 1.0
    explore_counter = 0
    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= hard_deadline or (now - start) >= seconds: break
        progress = min(1.0, (now - start) / max(1e-6, seconds))
        temp = temp0 * (temp1 / temp0) ** progress
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Gsize)
        C = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, Gsize)
        C = C[:L]
        Mc = float(C.max())
        S = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(Mc) or S <= 0:
            break
        sfft = (2.0 * n * Mc) / (S * S)
        if np.isfinite(sfft) and sfft < best_fft_sc - 1e-9:
            best_fft_sc = sfft
            best_fft_a = a.copy()
        with np.errstate(under='ignore', over='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
            z = temp * (C - Mc)
            ez = np.exp(z)
        ssum = float(ez.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
            logsum = 0.0
        else:
            w = ez / ssum
            logsum = math.log(ssum)
        Msm = Mc + logsum / temp
        wf = w[::-1]
        fw = np.fft.rfft(wf, Gsize)
        conv2 = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, Gsize)
        conv2 = conv2[:3*n-2]
        dM = 2.0 * conv2[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(dM)):
            break
        step += 1
        m = 0.9*m + 0.1*dM
        v = 0.999*v + 0.001*(dM*dM)
        mb = m / (1.0 - 0.9**step)
        vb = v / (1.0 - 0.999**step)
        lr = (lr0 * (lr1 / lr0) ** progress) * lr_mult
        a = _project_capsafe(a - lr * mb / (np.sqrt(vb) + 1e-8), target, 900.0)
        # exact eval periodic
        if (step % 300 == 0) or (now - last_exact > 1.5):
            sc = _eval_exact(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < holder[1]:
                holder[0] = a.copy(); holder[1] = sc
                last_exact = time.monotonic()
            else:
                # check best_fft maybe every other? maybe after 4 no improve
                scb = _eval_exact(best_fft_a)
                if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < holder[1]:
                    holder[0] = best_fft_a.copy(); holder[1] = scb
                    a = holder[0].copy()
                    last_exact = time.monotonic()
        # optional restart if exact eval too frequent? not.
    sc = _eval_exact(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < holder[1]: holder[0]=a.copy(); holder[1]=sc
    scb = _eval_exact(best_fft_a)
    if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < holder[1]: holder[0]=best_fft_a.copy(); holder[1]=scb
    return holder[0].copy()
```

```python
def lp_candidate(f, K=200, max_sec=20):
    if linprog is None or time_left < max_sec+30: return None
    C = conv f*f via FFT Fsize
    # active top K
    active = np.argsort(C)[-K:]
    active = np.unique(active)  # maybe size< K
    A = np.zeros((len(active), n))
    for r,j in enumerate(active): ...
    c = zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1
    A_ub = np.hstack((A, -np.eye(len(active))))
    b_ub = zeros(len(active))
    A_eq = zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1
    b_eq = [target]
    bounds = [(0,None)]*n + [(0,None)]
    tlimit = max(5, min(max_sec, (hard_deadline - now) - 60))
    if tlimit <=5: return None
    res=linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': tlimit})
    if res is not None and res.success:
       g=res.x[:n]
       g=np.clip(g,0,None)
       s=g.sum()
       if s>0: g*=target/s
       return _project_capsafe(g,target,900)
    return None
```

Let's code `_make_weights(C, delta)` inside run_direct to avoid repetition.
```python
def _weights(C, delta):
    Mc = C.max()
    with np.errstate(under='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
        z = (C - Mc) / delta
        ez = np.exp(z)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if not finite or s <=0:
       w=zeros; w[argmax]=1
    else:
       w=ez/s
       if w.sum()!=1 due nan: ...
    return w, Mc
```

Let's rewrite `_project_capsafe` robust:
```python
def _project_capsafe(v, s, cap=900.0):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy().ravel()
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, cap)
    out = _project_simplex(v, s)
    for _ in range(8):
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(out)):
            out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, cap)
        out = _project_simplex(out, s)
        if out.max() <= cap:
            break
    if out.max() > cap or not np.all(np.isfinite(out)) or abs(out.sum()-s) > 1e-3*max(1,s):
        # uniform fallback if cap allows
        out = np.full(v.size, s / max(1, v.size), dtype=np.float64)
        if out.max() > cap:
            # cannot? reduce? but target/n likely <=1; if n huge > cap? use cap?
            out = np.clip(out, 0, cap)
    return out
```
Uniform fallback sum may not equal s after clip if cap < s/n. But s/n=1 for target n, cap>1. OK.

But if input clipped sum may be less than target, theta negative and all coords increase by theta; capped coords may exceed. The loop fixes. Usually one or two.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def _p2(l):
    l = int(l)
    if l <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << ((l - 1).bit_length())


def _project_simplex(v, s):
    """Euclidean projection onto {x >= 0, sum(x) = s}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64) > (cssv - s))[0]

    if idx.size == 0:
        theta = (float(cssv[-1]) - s) / float(n)
        out = v - theta
    else:
        i = int(idx[-1])
        theta = (float(cssv[i]) - s) / float(i + 1)
        out = v - theta
        out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)

    out = np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)
    so = float(out.sum())
    if so > 0.0 and np.isfinite(so):
        out *= s / so
    else:
        out = np.full(n, s / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return out


def _project_capsafe(v, s, cap=1000.0):
    """Project to simplex and keep entries within [0, cap] as far as possible."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, cap)

    out = _project_simplex(v, s)
    for _ in range(10):
        out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, cap)
        out = _project_simplex(out, s)
        if out.size and float(out.max()) <= cap:
            break

    if (not np.all(np.isfinite(out))) or (out.size == 0) or abs(float(out.sum()) - s) > 1e-3 * max(1.0, s) or float(out.max()) > cap:
        out = np.full(n, s / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, cap)
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    reserve = min(35.0, max(2.0, 0.10 * budget))
    hard_deadline = t0 + budget - reserve

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Load the supplied parent construction only through the runtime symbol.
    base = None
    try:
        b = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if b.size and np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
            base = b
    except Exception:
        base = None

    if base is None:
        base = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64)

    # Safety: if an abnormal parent length is supplied, downsample to a length
    # that remains practical for the evaluator's dense convolution.
    MAX_N = 12000
    if base.size > MAX_N:
        m = MAX_N
        k = int(math.ceil(base.size / float(m)))
        base = base[:k * m].reshape(m, k).mean(axis=1)

    n = int(base.size)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 1
        base = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    Fsize = _p2(L)
    Gsize = _p2(3 * n - 2)
    target = float(n)
    cap = 1000.0

    def time_left():
        return max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic())

    def _eval_exact(a):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            return np.inf
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, cap)
        if float(a.sum()) < 0.01:
            return np.inf
        try:
            s = float(evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()))
            return s if np.isfinite(s) else np.inf
        except Exception:
            return np.inf

    def _score_fast(a):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        S = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
            return np.inf
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Fsize)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, Fsize)[:L]
        M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            return np.inf
        if M < 0.0:
            M = 0.0
        return float(2.0 * n * M / (S * S))

    def _conv_full_fast(a):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Fsize)
        return np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, Fsize)[:L]

    def _make_weights(C, delta):
        C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64)
        Mc = float(C.max())
        if delta <= 0.0:
            delta = 1e-12
        with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            z = (C - Mc) / delta
            ez = np.exp(z)
        ssum = float(ez.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(C.size, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = ez / ssum
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)) or float(w.sum()) <= 0.0:
                w = np.zeros(C.size, dtype=np.float64)
                w[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
        # If roundoff made several exact pins equally tight, keep them all.
        if float(w.sum()) > 0.0:
            tol = max(1e-7, 1e-10 * max(1.0, abs(Mc)))
            tie = np.where(C >= Mc - tol)[0]
            if tie.size > 1 and float((w[tie] > 0).sum()) <= 2:
                w[tie] = 0.0
                w[tie] = 1.0 / float(tie.size)
        return w, Mc

    holder = [None, np.inf]

    def _consider(a, force=False):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            return
        sc = _eval_exact(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and (force or sc < holder[1] - 1e-12):
            holder[0] = a.copy()
            holder[1] = float(sc)

    parent = _project_capsafe(base, target, cap)
    uniform = np.full(n, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    _consider(parent)
    _consider(uniform)
    if holder[0] is None:
        holder[0] = uniform.copy()
        holder[1] = _eval_exact(uniform)

    def run_direct(a0, seconds, width_start, width_end, width_mode, lr0, lr1, tag=""):
        a = _project_capsafe(np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).copy(), target, cap)
        _consider(a)
        sec = min(float(seconds), max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0))
        if sec <= 0.0:
            return a

        m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        step = 0
        start = time.monotonic()
        last_exact = start - 10.0
        best_fft_sc = _score_fast(a)
        best_fft_a = a.copy()
        lr_mult = 1.0
        no_improve = 0
        eval_gap = 2.5 if n <= 12000 else 5.0
        step_gap = 350 if n <= 12000 else 700

        while True:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= hard_deadline - 5.0 or (now - start) >= sec:
                break
            progress = min(1.0, (now - start) / max(1e-9, sec))

            if width_mode == "rel":
                frac = width_start + (width_end - width_start) * progress
                delta = max(1e-12, frac * max(1.0, float(a.sum()) / max(1, n)))
            else:
                delta = max(1e-12, width_start + (width_end - width_start) * progress)

            fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Gsize)
            C = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, Gsize)[:L]
            Mc = float(C.max())
            S = float(a.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(Mc) or not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
                break

            sfft = float(2.0 * n * Mc / (S * S))
            if np.isfinite(sfft) and sfft < best_fft_sc - 1e-12:
                best_fft_sc = sfft
                best_fft_a = a.copy()

            w, _ = _make_weights(C, delta)
            wf = w[::-1]
            fw = np.fft.rfft(wf, Gsize)
            conv2 = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, Gsize)[:3 * n - 2]
            dM = 2.0 * conv2[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
            dM = dM - float(dM.mean())

            if (not np.all(np.isfinite(dM))) or (float(dM.max()) - float(dM.min()) < 1e-12):
                # Symmetric/flat local direction; perturb slightly so projection
                # can continue without being stuck on a constant gradient.
                a = _project_capsafe(a + 0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n), target, cap)
                continue

            step += 1
            m = 0.90 * m + 0.10 * dM
            v = 0.999 * v + 0.001 * (dM * dM)
            mb = m / (1.0 - 0.9 ** step)
            vb = v / (1.0 - 0.999 ** step)
            lr = (lr0 * (lr1 / lr0) ** progress) * lr_mult
            a = _project_capsafe(a - lr * mb / (np.sqrt(vb) + 1e-8), target, cap)

            if (step % step_gap == 0) or ((now - last_exact) > eval_gap):
                sc = _eval_exact(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < holder[1] - 1e-12:
                    holder[0] = a.copy()
                    holder[1] = float(sc)
                    no_improve = 0
                    lr_mult = min(1.0, lr_mult * 1.25)
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
                    if no_improve >= 3:
                        lr_mult *= 0.65
                        no_improve = 0
                last_exact = time.monotonic()

        sc = _eval_exact(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < holder[1] - 1e-12:
            holder[0] = a.copy()
            holder[1] = float(sc)
        scb = _eval_exact(best_fft_a)
        if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < holder[1] - 1e-12:
            holder[0] = best_fft_a.copy()
            holder[1] = float(scb)
        return holder[0].copy() if holder[0] is not None else a

    def optimize_g(f, seconds, lr0=0.02, lr1=0.001, tag=""):
        """Approximate solve of the linear inner problem min ||f*g||_inf,
        g >= 0, sum(g) = target.
        """
        f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        g = _project_capsafe(f.copy(), target, cap)
        sec = min(float(seconds), max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0))
        if sec <= 0.0:
            return g

        ff = np.fft.rfft(f, Gsize)
        m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        step = 0
        start = time.monotonic()
        best_g = g.copy()
        best_J = np.inf

        while True:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= hard_deadline - 5.0 or (now - start) >= sec:
                break
            progress = min(1.0, (now - start) / max(1e-9, sec))
            frac = 0.25 + (0.02 - 0.25) * progress
            y = np.fft.irfft(ff * np.fft.rfft(g, Gsize), Gsize)[:L]
            Mc = float(y.max())
            if not np.isfinite(Mc):
                break
            if Mc < best_J:
                best_J = Mc
                best_g = g.copy()

            delta = max(1e-12, frac * max(1.0, Mc))
            w, _ = _make_weights(y, delta)
            wf = w[::-1]
            fw = np.fft.rfft(wf, Gsize)
            conv2 = np.fft.irfft(ff * fw, Gsize)[:3 * n - 2]
            D = conv2[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
            D = D - float(D.mean())

            if (not np.all(np.isfinite(D))) or (float(D.max()) - float(D.min()) < 1e-12):
                g = _project_capsafe(g + 0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n), target, cap)
                continue

            step += 1
            m = 0.90 * m + 0.10 * D
            v = 0.999 * v + 0.001 * (D * D)
            mb = m / (1.0 - 0.9 ** step)
            vb = v / (1.0 - 0.999 ** step)
            lr = lr0 * (lr1 / lr0) ** progress
            g = _project_capsafe(g - lr * mb / (np.sqrt(vb) + 1e-8), target, cap)

        return best_g

    def mix_update(f, g, alphas=(1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1)):
        f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        sf = _score_fast(f)
        best_try = None
        best_fast = sf
        trials = []

        for alpha in alphas:
            h = (1.0 - float(alpha)) * f + float(alpha) * g
            h = _project_capsafe(h, target, cap)
            sc = _score_fast(h)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_fast - 1e-12:
                best_fast = sc
                best_try = h.copy()
                trials.append((sc, h))

        # Exact-check the best few fast trials; this is cheap enough.
        if trials:
            trials.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
            for _, h in trials[:3]:
                sce = _eval_exact(h)
                if np.isfinite(sce):
                    holder_local = h if (holder[0] is None or sce < holder[1] - 1e-12) else holder[0]
                    holder_score = sce if holder_local is h else holder[1]
                    if holder_local is h:
                        holder[0] = h.copy()
                        holder[1] = float(sce)
        return best_try if best_try is not None else f

    def lp_candidate(f, K=160, max_sec=20):
        """Restricted active-set LP candidate for the inner minmax problem."""
        if linprog is None:
            return None
        tnow = time.monotonic()
        avail = hard_deadline - tnow - 35.0
        if avail < 12.0:
            return None
        K = int(min(max(32, K), 320))

        C = _conv_full_fast(f)
        active = np.argpartition(C, -K)[-K:]
        active = np.unique(active)
        K = int(active.size)
        if K <= 0:
            return None

        A = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
        fflat = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
        for r, j in enumerate(active):
            j = int(j)
            lo = max(0, j - n + 1)
            hi = min(j, n - 1)
            if hi >= lo:
                start = j - lo
                end = j - hi
                if end > 0:
                    A[r, lo:hi + 1] = fflat[start:end - 1:-1]
                else:
                    A[r, lo:hi + 1] = fflat[start::-1]

        c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        c[-1] = 1.0
        A_ub = np.hstack((A, -np.eye(K, dtype=np.float64)))
        b_ub = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
        b_eq = np.array([target], dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * (n + 1)

        tlimit = max(5.0, min(float(max_sec), hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0))
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c,
                A_ub=A_ub,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True, "time_limit": tlimit},
            )
        except Exception:
            return None

        if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
            return None
        g = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
        s = float(g.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            return None
        g *= target / s
        return _project_capsafe(g, target, cap)

    # ----------------------- search schedule -----------------------
    # Main broad-to-moderate direct polishing of the parent construction.
    if time_left() > 60.0:
        run_direct(
            parent,
            time_left() * 0.50,
            0.25,
            0.04,
            "rel",
            0.020,
            0.003,
            tag="main",
        )

    # A few cheap exploratory basins if time allows.
    if time_left() > 130.0:
        base_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        seeds = []
        seeds.append(_project_capsafe(1.0 + 0.9 * (2.0 * base_x - 1.0) ** 2, target, cap))
        seeds.append(_project_capsafe(1.0 + 0.7 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * 3.0 * base_x), target, cap))
        seeds.append(_project_capsafe(holder[0] + 0.04 * rng.standard_normal(n), target, cap))
        seed_sec = min(28.0, max(6.0, time_left() * 0.022))
        for sd in seeds:
            if time_left() < 70.0:
                break
            run_direct(sd, seed_sec, 0.30, 0.05, "rel", 0.018, 0.002, tag="seed")

    # Inner minmax / LP direction updates.
    outer = 0
    while time_left() > 180.0 and outer < 4:
        outer += 1
        cycle_start_score = holder[1]
        f = holder[0].copy() if holder[0] is not None else parent.copy()

        gsec = min(32.0, max(8.0, time_left() * 0.12))
        if gsec >= 8.0:
            g = optimize_g(f, gsec)
            mix_update(f, g)

        if time_left() > 210.0:
            f_now = holder[0].copy() if holder[0] is not None else f
            lg = lp_candidate(f_now, K=160, max_sec=22)
            if lg is not None:
                mix_update(f_now, lg)

        if holder[1] < cycle_start_score - 1e-10:
            continue
        break

    # Final high-precision polishing of the best exact candidate.
    if time_left() > 35.0:
        run_direct(
            holder[0] if holder[0] is not None else parent,
            max(0.0, time_left() - 20.0),
            2.0,
            0.05,
            "abs",
            0.004,
            0.0003,
            tag="final",
        )

    # ----------------------- final validation ---------------------
    a_final = holder[0] if holder[0] is not None else parent
    a_final = _project_capsafe(np.asarray(a_final, dtype=np.float64).copy(), target, cap)
    sc_final = _eval_exact(a_final)

    # If final projection changed the score, revert to the stored best if it is better.
    if (not np.isfinite(sc_final)) or (holder[0] is not None and sc_final > holder[1] + 1e-10):
        a_alt = holder[0] if holder[0] is not None else a_final
        a_alt = _project_capsafe(np.asarray(a_alt, dtype=np.float64).copy(), target, cap)
        sc_alt = _eval_exact(a_alt)
        if np.isfinite(sc_alt) and (not np.isfinite(sc_final) or sc_alt < sc_final):
            a_final = a_alt
            sc_final = sc_alt

    # Last-resort valid finite nonnegative sequence.
    if a_final is None or a_final.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a_final)) or _eval_exact(a_final) == np.inf:
        a_final = _project_capsafe(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), target, cap)
    if a_final.size == 0:
        a_final = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    out = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(a_final, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
    if (not out) or any((not np.isfinite(x)) or x < 0.0 for x in out):
        out = [1.0] * max(1, n)

    return out
```
